if (st.search && !this.data[name]) { st.search = ''; st.results = []; st.highlightIndex = -1; st.noResults = false; st.patternError = ''; if (this.isFieldRequired && this.isFieldRequired(name) && this.validateField) this.validateField(name); } this.asClose(name); }, asHasUnselectedText: function (name) { var st = this.asState[name]; return !!(st && st.search && !this.data[name]); }, asFocus: function (name) { var st = this.asState[name];
